_source line 3 · 2026-05-19T13:51:09.673Z_

[Director 指令] 几个修正和重新规划：

**数字修正**（Reviewer + 之前的 M010）：
- Full-replacement baseline 是 **84.2%**（GPT-2 XL nucleus seed42），不是 85.9%（temp09）。你的汇报用了 85.9%，请修正。Delta 是 -15.1pp / -14.1pp。
- mix25 vs mix50 的 1pp 差异在 seed 噪声范围内（跨 seed 方差 2.04pp），**不要做方向性解读**。两者是 "comparable (~69-70%)"，不是 "反直觉"。

**westd-48862 已释放 → baselines 重新规划**：
- Min-K% 和 DetectGPT 进程随服务器释放丢失
- **不需要重新租服务器** — baselines 总计 ~5-6 GPU-h，等 14B 完成后（May 20 ~05:00-06:00 UTC）在 gpu-6000pro-2 跑即可
- 14B 完成后立即提交 3 个 baselines（Min-K% + DetectGPT + Binoculars），预计 May 20 中午前全部完成
- 确认 baseline 脚本和数据在 gpu-6000pro-2 上可用？如果之前只上传到 westd-48862，需要重新部署到 gpu-6000pro-2

**当前无 GPU 任务**，利用等待窗口做：
1. 确认 gpu-6000pro-2 上有 9-cell atlas 数据 + baselines 脚本
2. 如果没有，从 artifacts 部署过去，为明天 14B 完成后无缝衔接做准备

mean_surprisal (0.089 vs 0.088)\\n5. p75_ppl (0.069 vs 0.067)\\n\\n### Code contamination\\n| Depth | Baseline | Temp09 | Δ |\\n|-------|----------|--------|---|\\n| d1 | 5.0% | 7.7% | +2.7pp |\\n| d2 | 9.0% | 11.6% | +2.6pp |\\n| d3 | 12.0% | 13.6% | +1.6pp |\\n\\n### Analysis\\n1. **Depth signal robust across decoding strategies**: temp_only τ=0.9 achieves 76.25% 4-class acc (vs 78.15% baseline), only 1.9pp lower — well within the hypothesis that depth signal is decoding-strategy-agnostic.\\n2. **Ordinal structure preserved**: AUC ordering 0v1 > 1v3 > 1v2 > 2v3 identical to baseline, confirming the difficulty gradient d0≫d1>d2>d3 is robust.\\n3. **d2 remains the weak link**: d2 recall (0.554 in 3-class) is the lowest, consistent with baseline — d2 is the hardest to distinguish from both d1 and d3.\\n4. **Feature importance stable**: Top-5 features identical between strategies with near-identical importance weights, suggesting the same statistical signatures carry depth information regardless of decoding method.\\n5. **Higher code contamination**: Temp09 shows 2-3pp higher code-like content across depths, possibly because temperature-only sampling without top-p truncation preserves more of the distribution tail including code patterns. HF streaming/大文件下载不可靠，改用 hf-mirror.com 下载 parquet 可绕过（FineWeb 2GB/114s）\\n\\n## LLM Zero-Shot Failure Modes (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- 两个 LLM（DeepSeek-v3.2 36.2%, Qwen2.5-7B 30.4%）均远低于 RF 78.2%，验证 zero-shot 一般性劣势\\n- 不同 LLM 的 failure mode 截然不同：DeepSeek 几乎全猜 d0（recall 95.2%），Qwen 偏向 d1（recall 55.2%）但 d0 recall 仅 26.4%\\n- 共同点：都无法捕获深层递归信号（d2/d3 recall 均很低）\\n- 论文叙事：两个模型的不同 failure pattern 说明这不是 prompt 偏差问题，而是 LLM zero-shot 根本无法访问 depth 所需的统计特征（surprisal distribution）\\n\\n## Multi-τ Ablation 关键洞察 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Memorization-as-prerequisite 机制\\n- τ=1.1 崩溃的根因不是\\\"特征变了\\\"而是\\\"特征消失了\\\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 memorization），τ=1.1 时 loss 2.27→1.91→1.85（几乎不降）\\n- **论文含义**：depth signal 的存在前提是递归训练会改变模型——当温度太高时训练数据等同于随机噪声，模型不变，depth 无可探测\\n\\n### τ 鲁棒性范围\\n- τ ∈ [0.5, 0.9] 均 d1v2 AUC > 0.80，depth signal 不是单一温度伪影\\n- τ=0.7 是 d1v2 AUC 峰值（0.851），接近 nucleus p=0.95 的 0.861\\n- 这回答了 Reviewer R2-W3 的关切：depth signal 跨温度鲁棒，但有上界\\n\\n### Cross-τ 不可迁移性\\n- Cross-τ LOO mean acc=46.7%，与 cross-strategy LOO (59.1%) 一致但更差\\n- 原因：不同 τ 产生不同的 surprisal distribution 形状，feature space 不可直接对齐\\n- 与论文现有 \\\"strategy-dependent feature space\\\" 叙事一致，multi-τ 提供了同一 strategy（temperature）内部的细粒度证据\\n\\n## Instruction-Tuning 正则化发现 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### 核心发现（D032）\\n- 原假设错误：以为 IT 引入 U-shape → 实际 BASE 本身就有 U-shape\\n- 正确解读：IT **正则化** depth signal（使 AUC 单调递减），而非引入伪影\\n- 证据对比：BASE AUC d2v3=0.6815 (dip) → d3v4=0.73 (recovery) = U-shape; Instruct AUC 单调递减 0.949→0.877→0.771→0.716→0.618\\n- **论文含义**：IT 不是 confound 而是有利条件，移除了关于\\\"所有实验用 IT 模型是否引入系统偏差\\\"的审稿顾虑\\n\\n### EDD Bug 教训\\n- `llama3_eval.py` 硬编码阈值 0.55 而非项目标准 0.70，导致初始 EDD 报告为 \\\">5\\\" 而非正确的 EDD=3\\n- **规则**：任何 eval 脚本中的 EDD 阈值必须参数化并默认 0.70，不得硬编码\\n- 影响范围：仅 BASE 实验的 eval 路径受影响；cross_scale_llama3_8b_003 使用了正确阈值（验证通过）\\n\\n## Text Length Sensitivity Analysis (2026-05-17)\\n\\n### 实验配置\\n- 3 models (pythia, olmo, gpt2xl) × 3 strategies (nucleus, temp09, topk50) = 9 cells\\n- 50 samples/depth/cell, 4 depths, total 1800 samples\\n- Tokenizer/model: pythia-1.4b (CPU, float32)\\n- 截断长度: 50, 100, 200, 500 tokens\\n- RF 5-fold CV, 200 estimators, seed=42\\n\\n### 结果摘要\\n\\n| Truncation | 4-class Acc | 3-class Acc | d0 recall | d1 recall | d2 recall | d3 recall |\\n|-----------|------------|------------|-----------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\\n| 50 tokens | 51.5% | 65.0% | 82.9% | 47.2% | 28.0% | 47.8% |\\n| 100 tokens | 56.3% | 72.8% | 89.8% | 54.3% | 27.5% | 53.4% |\\n| 200 tokens | 60.4% | 74.9% | 92.0% | 56.0% | 35.3% | 57.0% |\\n| 500 tokens | 61.0% | 78.9% | 93.8% | 66.9% | 42.7% | 55.2% |\\n\\n### 关键洞察\\n- **50 tokens 已可检测 depth signal**：4-class 51.5% 显著高于 chance (25%)，d0 recall 82.9%\\n- **收益递减**：50→200 tokens 提升 ~9pp（51.5→60.4%），200→500 仅 +0.7pp（60.4→61.0%）\\n- **3-class（排除 d0）持续提升**：50→500 从 65.0% 到 78.9%，说明更长文本对区分深层 depth 更有价值\\n- **d2 始终最难**：即使 500 tokens 也仅 42.7% recall，是分类瓶颈\\n- **实用意义**：200 tokens 是性价比拐点，足够用于实际 depth estimation pipeline\\n\\n## qwen25_14b_scale_002 诊断 (2026-05-17)\\n- 失败原因：模型下载阶段 aria2 遇到 SSL/TLS 19-feature 并将此结果放 ablation\\n\\n## ⚠️ Accumulation 实验方法论错误 M008/M009 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n### 错误描述\\naccum_mix50 和 accum_mix25 两次实现都犯了**同一个根本性错误**：在特征空间做混合（把 d0 的 features 直接塞进 d1/d2/d3 类，保留 depth label）。这不是 accumulation，是标签污染。\\n- 结果（35.5% / 67.2%）是标签污染的伪影，不反映真实 accumulation degradation\\n- 两个实验 registry 已标记 conclusion=failed, notes=INVALIDATED\\n\\n### 正确 accumulation pipeline\\n真正的 accumulation 需要在**文本层面**混合，每个 depth 都需要完整的：\\n1. 构造混合训练语料（raw text: human + 前一 depth 的 synthetic text）\\n2. LoRA 微调 GPT-2 XL（从 base model 重新开始）\\n3. 用微调后模型生成新文本\\n4. 提取 19 个统计特征\\n5. 预计每个 depth ~2-3h（LoRA + generation），3 depths ≈ 6-9h\\n\\n### 教训\\n- **\\\"accumulation\\\" 不是特征空间操作**——它是训练数据层面的操作，涉及模型重新训练\\n- 如果实验 <1h 完成且不需要 GPU，说明实现有误\\n- 关键操作（accumulation、fine-tuning 等）的实现方案必须经 Reviewer 审查后再执行\\n\\n## Accumulation v3 反直觉发现 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n### mix25 > mix50（更少 human text → 更好分类）\\n- mix25（25% human + 75% synthetic）70.1% 4-class > mix50（50% human + 50% synthetic）69.1%，差距 +1.0pp\\n- 原因：更高的 synthetic 比例使每个 depth 的训练数据更接近 full-replacement 场景，recursive signature 更强、更容易区分\\n- **推翻假设**：\\\"更多 human data → 更好 depth signal\\\" 是错误的。实际关系是：更多 synthetic → 训练数据间的 depth-specific 差异更大 → 分类更容易\\n- **论文含义**：Discussion §6 需要修正叙事方向——accumulation 的关键变量不是 human text 的 \\\"稀释\\\" 效果，而是 synthetic text 比例如何影响 recursive training 的 memorization 强度\\n- **d2 是跨所有 accumulation 比例的一致瓶颈**（~42% recall），与 full-replacement 实验中 d2 最难的 pattern 一致，说明 d2 困难性是 depth signal 的内禀属性而非 accumulation 引入\\n\\n## MDD 计算发现 (2026-05-19)\\n- MDD = EDD 在所有 12 个 profile 上成立（AUC 严格单调递减，无 U 型）\\n- MDD 不提供超越 EDD 的额外信息——depth signal 衰减是纯累积性的\\n- 唯一非单调 profile: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（atlas 外），但所有 pairs < 0.70，MDD=EDD=0\\n- d4v5 AUC 跨模型收敛到 0.681-0.684（range 仅 0.003）→ signal 消失边界\\n\", \"total_chars\": 9283, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

崩溃的根因不是\\\\\\\"特征变了\\\\\\\"而是\\\\\\\"特征消失了\\\\\\\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\\\\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 memorization），τ=1.1 时 loss label）。这不是 accumulation，是标签污染。\\n- 结果（35.5% / 67.2%）是标签污染的伪影，不反映真实 accumulation degradation\\n- 两个实验 registry 已标记 conclusion=failed, notes=INVALIDATED\\n\\n### 正确 accumulation pipeline\\n真正的 accumulation 需要在**文本层面**混合，每个 depth 都需要完整的：\\n1. 构造混合训练语料（raw text: human + 前一 depth 的 synthetic text）\\n2. LoRA 微调 GPT-2 XL（从 base model 重新开始）\\n3. 用微调后模型生成新文本\\n4. 提取 19 个统计特征\\n5. 预计每个 depth ~2-3h（LoRA + generation），3 depths ≈ 6-9h\\n\\n### 教训\\n- **\\\"accumulation\\\" 不是特征空间操作**——它是训练数据层面的操作，涉及模型重新训练\\n- 如果实验 <1h 完成且不需要 GPU，说明实现有误\\n- 关键操作（accumulation、fine-tuning 等）的实现方案必须经 Reviewer 审查后再执行\\n\\n## Accumulation v3 反直觉发现 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n### mix25 > mix50（更少 human text → 更好分类）\\n- mix25（25% human + 75% synthetic）70.1% 4-class > mix50（50% human + 50% synthetic）69.1%，差距 +1.0pp\\n- 原因：更高的 synthetic 比例使每个 depth 的训练数据更接近 full-replacement 场景，recursive signature 更强、更容易区分\\n- **推翻假设**：\\\"更多 human data → 更好 depth signal\\\" 是错误的。实际关系是：更多 synthetic → 训练数据间的 depth-specific 差异更大 → 分类更容易\\n- **论文含义**：Discussion §6 需要修正叙事方向——accumulation 的关键变量不是 human text 的 \\\"稀释\\\" 效果，而是 synthetic text 比例如何影响 recursive training 的 memorization 强度\\n- **d2 是跨所有 accumulation 比例的一致瓶颈**（~42% recall），与 full-replacement 实验中 d2 最难的 pattern 一致，说明 d2 困难性是 depth signal 的内禀属性而非 accumulation 引入\\n\\n## MDD 计算发现 (2026-05-19)\\n- MDD = EDD 在所有 12 个 profile 上成立（AUC 严格单调递减，无 U 型）\\n- MDD 不提供超越 EDD 的额外信息——depth signal 衰减是纯累积性的\\n- 唯一非单调 profile: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（atlas 外），但所有 pairs < 0.70，MDD=EDD=0\\n- d4v5 AUC 跨模型收敛到 0.681-0.684（range 仅 0.003）→ signal 消失边界\\n\", \"total_chars\": 9283, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

1	# Rebuttal Templates — Recursive Generation Depth Estimation
2	
3	## W10: Cross-seed replication only covers 3/9 cells
4	
5	**Weakness summary.** The reviewer notes that cross-seed replication (seed=43) was conducted on only 3 of the 9 atlas cells, questioning the generalizability of the reported stability.
6	
7	**Core rebuttal.** We appreciate the concern regarding replication coverage. The three cells selected for seed=43 validation were chosen deliberately to span all three model families (Pythia, OLMo, GPT-2 XL), ensuring that the replication is not confined to a single architecture. Across these cells, the seed=43 results show directional consistency with the primary seed, with accuracy shifts ranging from +0.1 to +3.2 percentage points. Two of the three cells yield statistically significant agreement, as confirmed by non-overlapping 95% confidence intervals between the two seeds. Crucially, feature importance rankings and depth ordering remain consistent across seeds: the same top features (p90_ppl, var_surprisal, and other perplexity tail features) dominate regardless of random seed, and the ordinal depth hierarchy (d0 > d1 > d2 > d3 separability) is preserved in all three cells.
8	
9	**Supporting data.** Seed=43 accuracy deltas: +0.1 pp (Pythia), +1.8 pp (OLMo), +3.2 pp (GPT-2 XL nucleus). Two of three cells show non-overlapping CIs (p < 0.05 equivalent).
10	**Acknowledgment.** We agree that full 9-cell replication would strengthen the claim. We will include the remaining 6 cells in the camera-ready version and note this as an ongoing extension. The current 3-cell coverage, spanning all model families, provides reasonable evidence that seed sensitivity is low.
11	
12	
13	## W11: No algorithmic contribution
14	
15	**Weakness summary.** The reviewer observes that the paper uses off-the-shelf Random Forest with standard statistical features, and questions whether this constitutes sufficient contribution.
16	
17	**Core rebuttal.** We respectfully note that ARR review criteria explicitly recognize empirical contributions as a standalone category. ARR explicitly values empirical contributions that provide new understanding, not only algorithmic novelty. Our paper introduces the recursive generation depth estimation task itself, which has not been previously formalized. The core contributions are empirical: (1) demonstrating that depth signal exists and is detectable in statistical features, a non-obvious finding given that binary detectors (Min-K%, DetectGPT) achieve only 30-35% on the same task; (2) systematically mapping transfer feasibility across 9 model-strategy cells; and (3) proposing the EDD metric, which reveals a consistent convergence point (EDD_{0.70}=4) across all three model families. The deliberate choice of a simple classifier is methodologically motivated: it isolates the contribution of the features and task formulation from classifier complexity, establishing a clean baseline that future algorithmic work can build upon.
18	
19	**Supporting data.** Binary detector baselines: Min-K% 33.1%, DetectGPT 30.8% (near chance for 4-class). Our RF baseline: 85.9% in-distribution, 66.4% cross-model. EDD_{0.70}=4 is consistent across Pythia, OLMo, and GPT-2 XL. The 5-class extension (71-80%) further validates feature robustness.
20	
21	**Acknowledgment.** We agree that exploring purpose-built architectures (e.g., ordinal regression networks, depth-aware contrastive learning) is a promising direction, and we discuss this in Future Work. The current work prioritizes establishing the task, the baseline, and the empirical phenomena that motivate such algorithmic development.
22	
23	
24	## W14: Single LoRA configuration
25	
26	**Weakness summary.** The reviewer raises concern that only one LoRA configuration (r=16, alpha=32) was used, and that LoRA hyperparameters might influence the results.
27	
28	**Core rebuttal.** We appreciate this concern and clarify the role of LoRA in our pipeline. LoRA is used exclusively for recursive chain fine-tuning to generate text at each depth level; it is not a component of the classifier. The classifier operates on 19 statistical features (perplexity distribution, burstiness, repetition patterns, etc.) extracted from the generated text, using an off-the-shelf Random Forest. The question is therefore whether LoRA hyperparameters affect the statistical properties of the generated text in ways that would change depth separability. Our cross-model transfer results directly address this: training a classifier on one model's outputs and testing on another's (where each model underwent independent LoRA fine-tuning with different effective capacities due to different base model sizes) yields 66.4% accuracy, well above chance (25%). This demonstrates that the depth-correlated statistical features generalize across different fine-tuning configurations and model architectures, rather than being artifacts of a specific LoRA setup.
29	
30	**Supporting data.** Cross-model transfer: 66.4% overall (d1-d3: 55.4%). The three models (Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, GPT-2 XL 1.5B) differ substantially in architecture and parameter count, yet LoRA r=16 produces transferable depth features across all three. Feature importance rankings show high overlap across models (top-5 features shared in 8/9 cells).
31	
32	**Acknowledgment.** We agree that sweeping LoRA configurations (e.g., r in {4, 8, 16, 32}) would add robustness. We note this in Limitations and plan to include a LoRA sensitivity analysis in future work. However, the cross-model evidence suggests that depth features are robust to fine-tuning variation.
33	
34	
35	## W15: Prompted generation only validated on 1/9 cells
36	
37	**Weakness summary.** The reviewer notes that the prompted generation experiment (topic-seeded vs. BOS-seeded) was conducted on only 1 of 9 atlas cells (GPT-2 XL nucleus).
38	
39	**Core rebuttal.** We designed the prompted generation experiment as a proof-of-concept to address a specific question: does depth signal persist when generation is conditioned on topic prompts rather than BOS tokens? The single-cell result already provides affirmative evidence. The prompted-generation classifier achieves 69.5% accuracy (95% CI [68.9, 70.2]), which is 2.8x chance level (25%) and demonstrates that depth-correlated features are not artifacts of unconditional generation. Critically, the qualitative signature of depth signal is fully preserved: AUC shows monotonic decay with depth (same pattern as BOS-seeded), and the top contributing features are identical between BOS-seeded and prompted conditions. Validating auxiliary hypotheses on a single representative cell is common practice in empirical NLP; full matrix extension is reserved for the primary experiment where statistical power and generalizability claims are strongest.
40	
41	**Supporting data.** Prompted generation accuracy: 69.5% (vs. 84.3% BOS-seeded on the same cell per Table 2, delta = -14.8 pp). The 14.8 pp drop is expected, as topic conditioning reduces distributional variance, yet 69.5% remains strongly above chance. Monotonic AUC decay: d1 > d2 > d3, consistent with the BOS-seeded pattern across all 9 cells. Top features identical: p90_ppl (Gini importance top-2 in 9/9 cells), var_surprisal (SHAP top-2 in 8/9 cells), both from the perplexity tail feature family.
42	
43	**Acknowledgment.** We agree that extending prompted generation to the full 9-cell atlas would strengthen this finding. We plan to include this in an expanded version. The current single-cell result, combined with qualitative consistency (identical features and decay pattern), provides sufficient evidence for the proof-of-concept claim made in the paper.
44

Cross-strategy LOO d1-d3 accuracy (generalization.tex L95)\\n- **45.9%** (mean across 9 folds)\\n- Source: confusion matrices in `w6_cross_strategy_per_class_recall.json`\\n- Method: (d1_correct + d2_correct + d3_correct) / 15000 per fold, then averaged\\n- Note: 4-class mean was 59.1%; d0's near-perfect accuracy inflates it. Excluding d0 drops to 45.9%.\\n- Range across folds: 35.4% (pythia_topk50) to 59.1% (pythia_nucleus)\\n\\n### Artifact\\n- `artifacts/placeholder_values.json` — full breakdown with per-fold numbers\\n\\n## Downstream Curation PoC Insights (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### Oracle ≤ Unfiltered（反直觉发现）\\n- 完美 depth 筛选（oracle）反而不优于 unfiltered 全量数据：oracle_strict PPL 15.879 vs unfiltered 15.776（p=0.22）\\n- 原因推测：纯按 depth 筛选过度移除了有用的多样性，RF 的分类误差反而保留了有价值的变异\\n- **Framing 含义**：不能 claim \\\"RF approximates oracle\\\"，应 claim \\\"data efficiency\\\"（RF 用 44% 数据 ≈ 全量 PPL）\\n\\n### rf_moderate 统计检验偏差\\n- 我们的 t=-3.77 (p=0.02) vs Reviewer 手算 t=-5.58 (p=0.005)\\n- 其余 3 组对比偏差 <8%，此组偏差较大，以原始数据直接计算为准\\n- 定性结论一致（均显著），不影响 claim\\n\\n## ⚠️ Wild Data 解读约束 C004 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n**硬约束**：wild data RF 分类结果的 d1+ 预测是 OOD 假阳性率（FPR），不是 AI contamination 证据。\\n- C4 FPR 34%、FineWeb FPR 17%：差异来自 FineWeb 质量过滤去除了特征异常文本，不代表 C4 有更多 AI 内容\\n- C4 采集于 2019 年 Common Crawl，彼时几乎不存在 LLM 生成内容\\n- 论文中**禁止**说 \\\"contamination\\\"、\\\"AI-generated content detected\\\"\\n- 正确 framing：method limitation + calibration gap\\n- 教训：不要将分类器在 OOD 数据上的预测当作真实标签解读\\n\\n## Downstream Benchmark v2 结论 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n- d0_only 并非最优训练数据（PPL 5.65），d1_only 最低（5.55）\\n- PPL 差异极小（max 0.12），500 条训练不足以达到统计显著性\\n- **论文 framing**：exploratory/pilot analysis，放 Discussion 或 Appendix，claim 强度为 \\\"we observe that...\\\"\\n- 确定信号：train_loss 单调递减（d0 2.558 > d1 1.920 > d2 1.286），支持 \\\"递归生成降低信息量\\\"\\n- 不是 main contribution\\n\\n## Learned Estimator 结论翻转 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n- learned_estimator_001 (negative) → learned_estimator_002 (success)\\n- 根因：Proto NaN 导致 gpt2xl holdout 结果损坏，修复后 proto gpt2xl 68.35% > RF 66.4%\\n- XGBoost mean 67.18% 最强，Proto 单项最高（olmo 70.01%）\\n- Pythia holdout 对所有方法最难（61-64%），可能因 Pythia 文本分布与 olmo/gpt2xl 差异较大\\n- 教训：NaN/Inf 在距离度量中静默传播，需要在 cdist/softmax 管道中添加防御性 clamp\\n\\n## Feature Importance & RF Stability (2026-05-17)\\n\\n### RF Seed Robustness\\n- 3 seeds (42/123/456) mean 4-class accuracy: **66.48% ± 0.06%** — 种子对结果几乎无影响，RF 高度稳定\\n\\n### Feature Importance Ablation\\n- **Perplexity 组 (9 features) 单独达到 66.0%**，仅比全 19 特征低 0.5pp — 是绝对主导特征组\\n- Entropy/Surprisal 组 (5 features): 63.2%，贡献中等\\n- Repetition/Diversity 组 (5 features): 53.0%，最弱但仍显著高于 25% chance\\n- **Top-2 特征占总 importance 41%**：p90_ppl (22.4%) + var_surprisal (18.8%)\\n- mean_ppl 和 var_ppl importance 接近零（被 percentile 特征替代）\\n- **论文含义**：perplexity degradation is the dominant signal — 与 feature bottleneck 结论一致，更复杂分类器无法显著超越 RF 因为信息集中在少数 perplexity 特征\\n\\n## Wild Data Calibration 跨数据集一致性 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- Web-crawl FPR ~20% 是跨数据集一致的现象（C4=20.2%, FineWeb=19.5%, Δ<1pp），由模板/SEO 内容驱动\\n- 编辑质量文本（WikiText）FPR 稳定 0%\\n- 所有 web-crawl 误分类均为 d1（无 d2/d3 false positives），误分类程度温和\\n- AutoDL 代理下 HF streaming/大文件下载不可靠，改用 hf-mirror.com 下载 parquet 可绕过（FineWeb 2GB/114s）\\n\\n## LLM Zero-Shot Failure Modes (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- 两个 LLM（DeepSeek-v3.2 36.2%, Qwen2.5-7B 30.4%）均远低于 RF 78.2%，验证 zero-shot 一般性劣势\\n- 不同 LLM 的 failure mode 截然不同：DeepSeek 几乎全猜 d0（recall 95.2%），Qwen 偏向 d1（recall 55.2%）但 d0 recall 仅 26.4%\\n- 共同点：都无法捕获深层递归信号（d2/d3 recall 均很低）\\n- 论文叙事：两个模型的不同 failure pattern 说明这不是 prompt 偏差问题，而是 LLM zero-shot 根本无法访问 depth 所需的统计特征（surprisal distribution）\\n\\n## Multi-τ Ablation 关键洞察 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Memorization-as-prerequisite 机制\\n- τ=1.1 崩溃的根因不是\\\"特征变了\\\"而是\\\"特征消失了\\\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 memorization），τ=1.1 时 loss 2.27→1.91→1.85（几乎不降）\\n- **论文含义**：depth signal 的存在前提是递归训练会改变模型——当温度太高时训练数据等同于随机噪声，模型不变，depth 无可探测\\n\\n### τ 鲁棒性范围\\n- τ ∈ [0.5, 0.9] 均 d1v2 AUC > 0.80，depth signal 不是单一温度伪影\\n- τ=0.7 是 d1v2 AUC 峰值（0.851），接近 nucleus p=0.95 的 0.861\\n- 这回答了 Reviewer R2-W3 的关切：depth signal 跨温度鲁棒，但有上界\\n\\n### Cross-τ 不可迁移性\\n- Cross-τ LOO mean acc=46.7%，与 cross-strategy LOO (59.1%) 一致但更差\\n- 原因：不同 τ 产生不同的 surprisal distribution 形状，feature space 不可直接对齐\\n- 与论文现有 \\\"strategy-dependent feature space\\\" 叙事一致，multi-τ 提供了同一 strategy（temperature）内部的细粒度证据\\n\\n## Instruction-Tuning 正则化发现 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### 核心发现（D032）\\n- 原假设错误：以为 IT 引入 U-shape → 实际 BASE 本身就有 U-shape\\n- 正确解读：IT **正则化** depth signal（使 AUC 单调递减），而非引入伪影\\n- 证据对比：BASE AUC d2v3=0.6815 (dip) → d3v4=0.73 (recovery) = U-shape; Instruct AUC 单调递减 0.949→0.877→0.771→0.716→0.618\\n- **论文含义**：IT 不是 confound 而是有利条件，移除了关于\\\"所有实验用 IT 模型是否引入系统偏差\\\"的审稿顾虑\\n\\n### EDD Bug 教训\\n- `llama3_eval.py` 硬编码阈值 0.55 而非项目标准 0.70，导致初始 EDD 报告为 \\\">5\\\" 而非正确的 EDD=3\\n- **规则**：任何 eval 脚本中的 EDD 阈值必须参数化并默认 0.70，不得硬编码\\n- 影响范围：仅 BASE 实验的 eval 路径受影响；cross_scale_llama3_8b_003 使用了正确阈值（验证通过）\\n\\n## Text Length Sensitivity Analysis (2026-05-17)\\n\\n### 实验配置\\n- 3 models (pythia, olmo, gpt2xl) × 3 strategies (nucleus, temp09, topk50) = 9 cells\\n- 50 samples/depth/cell, 4 depths, total 1800 samples\\n- Tokenizer/model: pythia-1.4b (CPU, float32)\\n- 截断长度: 50, 100, 200, 500 tokens\\n- RF 5-fold CV, 200 estimators, seed=42\\n\\n### 结果摘要\\n\\n| Truncation | 4-class Acc | 3-class Acc | d0 recall | d1 recall | d2 recall | d3 recall |\\n|-----------|------------|------------|-----------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\\n| 50 tokens | 51.5% | 65.0% | 82.9% | 47.2% | 28.0% | 47.8% |\\n| 100 tokens | 56.3% | 72.8% | 89.8% | 54.3% | 27.5% | 53.4% |\\n| 200 tokens | 60.4% | 74.9% | 92.0% | 56.0% | 35.3% | 57.0% |\\n| 500 tokens | 61.0% | 78.9% | 93.8% | 66.9% | 42.7% | 55.2% |\\n\\n### 关键洞察\\n- **50 tokens 已可检测 depth signal**：4-class 51.5% 显著高于 chance (25%)，d0 recall 82.9%\\n- **收益递减**：50→200 tokens 提升 ~9pp（51.5→60.4%），200→500 仅 +0.7pp（60.4→61.0%）\\n- **3-class（排除 d0）持续提升**：50→500 从 65.0% 到 78.9%，说明更长文本对区分深层 depth 更有价值\\n- **d2 始终最难**：即使 500 tokens 也仅 42.7% recall，是分类瓶颈\\n- **实用意义**：200 tokens 是性价比拐点，足够用于实际 depth estimation pipeline\\n\\n## qwen25_14b_scale_002 诊断 (2026-05-17)\\n- 失败原因：模型下载阶段 aria2 遇到 SSL/TLS handshake failure，shard 5/8 下载中断导致脚本 EXIT_CODE=1\\n- 详细错误：`[SocketCore.cc:1018] errorCode=1 SSL/TLS handshake failure: The TLS connection was non-properly terminated.` — 下载 model-00005-of-00008.safetensors 时与 cas-bridge.xethub.hf.co 的 TLS 连接被异常终止\\n- 当前状态：shards 1-3 完整，shard 4 下载中（有 .aria2 控制文件），shards 5-8 缺失；磁盘空间充足（103GB 可用）\\n- 修复建议：重新提交实验即可，脚本已有 skip-existing 逻辑会跳过已下载的 shards；或在脚本中为 aria2 添加 `--max-tries=5 --retry-wait=10` 重试参数防止瞬态网络故障\\n\\n## ⚠️ Table 2 数据口径不一致 (2026-05-18)\\n\\n### 排查结论\\n- Table 2 主表 4-class 数字实际来自 **80/20 split + 100 trees + StandardScaler**，但 caption 声称 \\\"5-fold CV, 200 trees\\\"\\n- Table 2 括号 d1-d3 数字**无单一 JSON 来源**——在项目开发不同阶段分批计算，各 cell 可能用了不同 RF 配置\\n- Abstract 71.1% 使用口径 A（4-class RF, 5-fold CV, 200 trees 的 d1-d3 balanced accuracy），与 Table 2 实际口径不一致\\n\\n### 统一重跑（RF-200, 5-Fold CV, No Scaler）\\n- 全 9 cells 重跑完成，存于 `results/table2_rerun_5foldcv.json`\\n- Mean 4-class: 78.44% | Mean d1-d3 BalAcc: 71.63%\\n- 与旧 Table 2 主表差异 <1pp（大部分），最大差异 olmo_nucleus d1-d3 -2.4pp\\n- 待 Director 决策是否替换\\n\\n## 21-Feature Augmented RF 洞察 (2026-05-18)\\n\\n### Min-K% 是互补特征，DetectGPT 是冗余特征\\n- Min-K% K=20 score 加入 19-feature RF 后 rank #3（importance 0.0952），仅次于 p90_ppl 和 var_surprisal\\n- 说明 token-level log-prob 最低 K% 均值捕获了 passage-level 统计特征未完全覆盖的信息\\n- DetectGPT perturbation discrepancy rank #17/21（importance 0.0255），几乎无增量贡献\\n- **论文含义**：统计特征已捕获绝大部分 depth 判别信息；Min-K% 提供温和互补（+0.48pp），DetectGPT 完全冗余——强化 \\\"depth estimation ≠ binary detection\\\" 论点\\n- **决策待定**：是否将 20-feature (+Min-K%) 作为正式配置，还是保持 19-feature 并将此结果放 ablation\\n\\n## ⚠️ Accumulation 实验方法论错误 M008/M009 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n### 错误描述\\naccum_mix50 和 accum_mix25 两次实现都犯了**同一个根本性错误**：在特征空间做混合（把 d0 的 features 直接塞进 d1/d2/d3 类，保留 depth label）。这不是 accumulation，是标签污染。\\n- 结果（35.5% / 67.2%）是标签污染的伪影，不反映真实 accumulation degradation\\n- 两个实验 registry 已标记 conclusion=failed, notes=INVALIDATED\\n\\n### 正确 accumulation pipeline\\n真正的 accumulation 需要在**文本层面**混合，每个 depth 都需要完整的：\\n1. 构造混合训练语料（raw text: human + 前一 depth 的 synthetic text）\\n2. LoRA 微调 GPT-2 XL（从 base model 重新开始）\\n3. 用微调后模型生成新文本\\n4. 提取 19 个统计特征\\n5. 预计每个 depth ~2-3h（LoRA + generation），3 depths ≈ 6-9h\\n\\n### 教训\\n- **\\\"accumulation\\\" 不是特征空间操作**——它是训练数据层面的操作，涉及模型重新训练\\n- 如果实验 <1h 完成且不需要 GPU，说明实现有误\\n- 关键操作（accumulation、fine-tuning 等）的实现方案必须经 Reviewer 审查后再执行\\n\\n## Accumulation v3 反直觉发现 (2026-05-19)\\n\\n### mix25 > mix50（更少 human text → 更好分类）\\n- mix25（25% human + 75% synthetic）70.1% 4-class > mix50（50% human + 50% synthetic）69.1%，差距 +1.0pp\\n- 原因：更高的 synthetic 比例使每个 depth 的训练数据更接近 full-replacement 场景，recursive signature 更强、更容易区分\\n- **推翻假设**：\\\"更多 human data → 更好 depth signal\\\" 是错误的。实际关系是：更多 synthetic → 训练数据间的 depth-specific 差异更大 → 分类更容易\\n- **论文含义**：Discussion §6 需要修正叙事方向——accumulation 的关键变量不是 human text 的 \\\"稀释\\\" 效果，而是 synthetic text 比例如何影响 recursive training 的 memorization 强度\\n- **d2 是跨所有 accumulation 比例的一致瓶颈**（~42% recall），与 full-replacement 实验中 d2 最难的 pattern 一致，说明 d2 困难性是 depth signal 的内禀属性而非 accumulation 引入\\n\\n## MDD 计算发现 (2026-05-19)\\n- MDD = EDD 在所有 12 个 profile 上成立（AUC 严格单调递减，无 U 型）\\n- MDD 不提供超越 EDD 的额外信息——depth signal 衰减是纯累积性的\\n- 唯一非单调 profile: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（atlas 外），但所有 pairs < 0.70，MDD=EDD=0\\n- d4v5 AUC 跨模型收敛到 0.681-0.684（range 仅 0.003）→ signal 消失边界\\n\", \"total_chars\": 9283, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

binary detection\\\\\\\" 论点\\\\n- **决策待定**：是否将 20-feature (+Min-K%) 作为正式配置，还是保持 19-feature 并将此结果放 ablation\\\\n\\\\n## ⚠️ Accumulation 实验方法论错误 M008/M009 (2026-05-19)\\\\n\\\\n### 错误描述\\\\naccum_mix50 和 accum_mix25 两次实现都犯了**同一个根本性错误**：在特征空间做混合（把 d0 的 features 直接塞进 d1/d2/d3 类，保留 depth label）。这不是 accumulation，是标签污染。\\\\n- 结果（35.5% / 67.2%）是标签污染的伪影，不反映真实 accumulation degradation\\\\n- 两个实验 registry 已标记 conclusion=failed, notes=INVALIDATED\\\\n\\\\n### 正确 accumulation pipeline\\\\n真正的 accumulation 需要在**文本层面**混合，每个 depth 都需要完整的：\\\\n1. 构造混合训练语料（raw text: human + 前一 depth 的 synthetic text）\\\\n2. LoRA 微调 GPT-2 XL（从 base model 重新开始）\\\\n3. 用微调后模型生成新文本\\\\n4. 提取 19 个统计特征\\\\n5. 预计每个 depth ~2-3h（LoRA + generation），3 depths ≈ 6-9h\\\\n\\\\n### 教训\\\\n- **\\\\\\\"accumulation\\\\\\\" 不是特征空间操作**——它是训练数据层面的操作，涉及模型重新训练\\\\n- 如果实验 <1h 完成且不需要 GPU，说明实现有误\\\\n- 关键操作（accumulation、fine-tuning 等）的实现方案必须经 Reviewer 审查后再执行\\\\n\\\\n## Accumulation v3 反直觉发现 (2026-05-19)\\\\n\\\\n### mix25 > mix50（更少 human text → 更好分类）\\\\n- mix25（25% human + 75% synthetic）70.1% 4-class > mix50（50% human + 50% synthetic）69.1%，差距 +1.0pp\\\\n- 原因：更高的 synthetic 比例使每个 depth 的训练数据更接近 full-replacement 场景，recursive signature 更强、更容易区分\\\\n- **推翻假设**：\\\\\\\"更多 human data → 更好 depth signal\\\\\\\" 是错误的。实际关系是：更多 synthetic → 训练数据间的 depth-specific 差异更大 → 分类更容易\\\\n- **论文含义**：Discussion §6 需要修正叙事方向——accumulation 的关键变量不是 human text 的 \\\\\\\"稀释\\\\\\\" 效果，而是 synthetic text 比例如何影响 recursive training 的 memorization 强度\\\\n- **d2 是跨所有 accumulation 比例的一致瓶颈**（~42% recall），与 full-replacement 实验中 d2 最难的 pattern 一致，说明 d2 困难性是 depth signal 的内禀属性而非 accumulation 引入\\\\n\\\\n## MDD 计算发现 (2026-05-19)\\\\n- MDD = EDD 在所有 12 个 profile 上成立（AUC 严格单调递减，无 U 型）\\\\n- MDD 不提供超越 EDD 的额外信息——depth signal 衰减是纯累积性的\\\\n- 唯一非单调 profile: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（atlas 外），但所有 pairs < 0.70，MDD=EDD=0\\\\n- d4v5 AUC 跨模型收敛到 0.681-0.684（range 仅 0.003）→ signal 消失边界\\\\n\\\", \\\"total_chars\\\": 9283, \\\"truncated\\\": false}, \\\"error\\\": null, \\\"metadata\\\": {}}\"}",

Excluding d0 drops to 45.9%.\\n- Range across folds: 35.4% (pythia_topk50) to 59.1% (pythia_nucleus)\\n\\n### Artifact\\n- `artifacts/placeholder_values.json` — full breakdown with per-fold numbers\\n\\n## Downstream Curation PoC Insights (2026-05-15)\\n\\n### Oracle ≤ Unfiltered（反直觉发现）\\n- 完美 depth 筛选（oracle）反而不优于 unfiltered 全量数据：oracle_strict PPL 15.879 vs unfiltered 15.776（p=0.22）\\n- 原因推测：纯按 depth 筛选过度移除了有用的多样性，RF 的分类误差反而保留了有价值的变异\\n- **Framing 含义**：不能 claim \\\"RF approximates oracle\\\"，应 claim \\\"data efficiency\\\"（RF 用 44% 数据 ≈ 全量 PPL）\\n\\n### rf_moderate 统计检验偏差\\n- 我们的 t=-3.77 (p=0.02) vs Reviewer 手算 t=-5.58 (p=0.005)\\n- 其余 3 组对比偏差 <8%，此组偏差较大，以原始数据直接计算为准\\n- 定性结论一致（均显著），不影响 claim\\n\\n## ⚠️ Wild Data 解读约束 C004 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n**硬约束**：wild data RF 分类结果的 d1+ 预测是 OOD 假阳性率（FPR），不是 AI contamination 证据。\\n- C4 FPR 34%、FineWeb FPR 17%：差异来自 FineWeb 质量过滤去除了特征异常文本，不代表 C4 有更多 AI 内容\\n- C4 采集于 2019 年 Common Crawl，彼时几乎不存在 LLM 生成内容\\n- 论文中**禁止**说 \\\"contamination\\\"、\\\"AI-generated content detected\\\"\\n- 正确 framing：method limitation + calibration gap\\n- 教训：不要将分类器在 OOD 数据上的预测当作真实标签解读\\n\\n## Downstream Benchmark v2 结论 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n- d0_only 并非最优训练数据（PPL 5.65），d1_only 最低（5.55）\\n- PPL 差异极小（max 0.12），500 条训练不足以达到统计显著性\\n- **论文 framing**：exploratory/pilot analysis，放 Discussion 或 Appendix，claim 强度为 \\\"we observe that...\\\"\\n- 确定信号：train_loss 单调递减（d0 2.558 > d1 1.920 > d2 1.286），支持 \\\"递归生成降低信息量\\\"\\n- 不是 main contribution\\n\\n## Learned Estimator 结论翻转 (2026-05-17)\\n\\n- learned_estimator_001 (negative) → learned_estimator_002 (success)\\n- 根因：Proto NaN 导致 gpt2xl holdout 结果损坏，修复后 proto gpt2xl 68.35% > RF 66.4%\\n- XGBoost mean 67.18% 最强，Proto 单项最高（olmo 70.01%）\\n- Pythia holdout 对所有方法最难（61-64%），可能因 Pythia 文本分布与 olmo/gpt2xl 差异较大\\n- 教训：NaN/Inf 在距离度量中静默传播，需要在 cdist/softmax 管道中添加防御性 clamp\\n\\n## Feature Importance & RF Stability (2026-05-17)\\n\\n### RF Seed Robustness\\n- 3 seeds (42/123/456) mean 4-class accuracy: **66.48% ± 0.06%** — 种子对结果几乎无影响，RF 高度稳定\\n\\n### Feature Importance Ablation\\n- **Perplexity 组 (9 features) 单独达到 66.0%**，仅比全 19 特征低 0.5pp — 是绝对主导特征组\\n- Entropy/Surprisal 组 (5 接近零（被 percentile 特征替代）\\n- **论文含义**：perplexity degradation is the dominant signal — 与 feature bottleneck 结论一致，更复杂分类器无法显著超越 RF 因为信息集中在少数 perplexity 特征\\n\\n## Wild Data Calibration 跨数据集一致性 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- Web-crawl FPR ~20% 是跨数据集一致的现象（C4=20.2%, FineWeb=19.5%, Δ<1pp），由模板/SEO 内容驱动\\n- 编辑质量文本（WikiText）FPR 稳定 0%\\n- 所有 web-crawl 误分类均为 d1（无 d2/d3 false positives），误分类程度温和\\n- AutoDL 代理下 HF streaming/大文件下载不可靠，改用 hf-mirror.com 下载 parquet 可绕过（FineWeb 2GB/114s）\\n\\n## LLM Zero-Shot Failure Modes (2026-05-16)\\n\\n- 两个 LLM（DeepSeek-v3.2 36.2%, Qwen2.5-7B 30.4%）均远低于 RF 78.2%，验证 zero-shot 一般性劣势\\n- 不同 LLM 的 failure mode 截然不同：DeepSeek 几乎全猜 d0（recall 95.2%），Qwen 偏向 d1（recall 55.2%）但 d0 recall 仅 26.4%\\n- 共同点：都无法捕获深层递归信号（d2/d3 recall 均很低）\\n- 论文叙事：两个模型的不同 failure pattern 说明这不是 prompt 偏差问题，而是 LLM zero-shot 根本无法访问 depth 所需的统计特征（surprisal distribution）\\n\\n## Multi-τ Ablation 关键洞察 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### Memorization-as-prerequisite 机制\\n- τ=1.1 崩溃的根因不是\\\"特征变了\\\"而是\\\"特征消失了\\\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 memorization），τ=1.1 时 loss 2.27→1.91→1.85（几乎不降）\\n- **论文含义**：depth signal 的存在前提是递归训练会改变模型——当温度太高时训练数据等同于随机噪声，模型不变，depth 无可探测\\n\\n### τ 鲁棒性范围\\n- τ ∈ [0.5, 0.9] 均 d1v2 AUC > 0.80，depth signal 不是单一温度伪影\\n- τ=0.7 是 d1v2 AUC 峰值（0.851），接近 nucleus p=0.95 的 0.861\\n- 这回答了 Reviewer R2-W3 的关切：depth signal 跨温度鲁棒，但有上界\\n\\n### Cross-τ 不可迁移性\\n- Cross-τ LOO mean acc=46.7%，与 cross-strategy LOO (59.1%) 一致但更差\\n- 原因：不同 τ 产生不同的 surprisal distribution 形状，feature space 不可直接对齐\\n- 与论文现有 \\\"strategy-dependent feature space\\\" 叙事一致，multi-τ 提供了同一 strategy（temperature）内部的细粒度证据\\n\\n## Instruction-Tuning 正则化发现 (2026-05-16)\\n\\n### 核心发现（D032）\\n- 原假设错误：以为 IT 引入 U-shape → 实际 BASE 本身就有 U-shape\\n- 正确解读：IT **正则化** depth signal（使 AUC 单调递减），而非引入伪影\\n- 证据对比：BASE AUC d2v3=0.6815 (dip) → d3v4=0.73 (recovery) = U-shape; Instruct AUC 单调递减 0.949→0.877→0.771→0.716→0.618\\n- **论文含义**：IT 不是 confound 而是有利条件，移除了关于\\\"所有实验用 IT 模型是否引入系统偏差\\\"的审稿顾虑\\n\\n### EDD Bug 教训\\n- `llama3_eval.py` 硬编码阈值 0.55 而非项目标准 0.70，导致初始 EDD 报告为 \\\">5\\\" 而非正确的 EDD=3\\n- **规则**：任何 eval 脚本中的 EDD 阈值必须参数化并默认 Memorization-as-prerequisite 机制\n- τ=1.1 崩溃的根因不是\"特征变了\"而是\"特征消失了\"：高温使 token 分布过于随机，模型无法学习自身输出模式，depth-dependent 的 memorization 效应不再发生\n- 训练 loss 是直接证据：τ=0.5 时 loss 2.27→0.42→0.16（快速 产生不同的 surprisal distribution 形状，feature space 不可直接对齐\n- 与论文现有 \"strategy-dependent feature space\" 叙事一致，multi-τ 提供了同一 strategy（temperature）内部的细粒度证据\n\n## Instruction-Tuning 正则化发现 (2026-05-16)\n\n### tokens\n- RF 5-fold CV, 200 estimators, seed=42\n\n### 结果摘要\n\n| Truncation | 4-class Acc | 3-class Acc | d0 recall | d1 recall | d2 recall | d3 recall |\n|-----------|------------|------------|-----------|-----------|-----------|-----------|\n| 50 tokens | 51.5% | 65.0% | 82.9% | 47.2% | 28.0% | 47.8% |\n| 100 tokens | 56.3% | 72.8% | 89.8% | 54.3% | 27.5% | 53.4% |\n| 200 tokens | 60.4% | 74.9% | 92.0% | 56.0% | 35.3% | 57.0% |\n| 500 tokens | 61.0% | 78.9% | 93.8% | 66.9% | 42.7% | 55.2% |\n\n### 关键洞察\n- **50 tokens 已可检测 depth signal**：4-class 51.5% 显著高于 chance (25%)，d0 recall 82.9%\n- **收益递减**：50→200 tokens 提升 ~9pp（51.5→60.4%），200→500 仅 +0.7pp（60.4→61.0%）\n- **3-class（排除 d0）持续提升**：50→500 从 65.0% 到 78.9%，说明更长文本对区分深层 depth 更有价值\n- **d2 始终最难**：即使 500 tokens 也仅 42.7% recall，是分类瓶颈\n- **实用意义**：200 tokens 是性价比拐点，足够用于实际 depth estimation pipeline\n\n## qwen25_14b_scale_002 诊断 (2026-05-17)\n- 失败原因：模型下载阶段 aria2 遇到 SSL/TLS handshake failure，shard 5/8 下载中断导致脚本 EXIT_CODE=1\n- 详细错误：`[SocketCore.cc:1018] errorCode=1 SSL/TLS handshake failure: The TLS connection was non-properly terminated.` — 下载 model-00005-of-00008.safetensors 时与 cas-bridge.xethub.hf.co 的 TLS 连接被异常终止\n- 当前状态：shards 1-3 完整，shard 4 下载中（有 .aria2 控制文件），shards 5-8 缺失；磁盘空间充足（103GB 可用）\n- 修复建议：重新提交实验即可，脚本已有 skip-existing 逻辑会跳过已下载的 shards；或在脚本中为 aria2 添加 `--max-tries=5 --retry-wait=10` 重试参数防止瞬态网络故障\n\n## ⚠️ Table 2 数据口径不一致 (2026-05-18)\n\n### 排查结论\n- Table 2 主表 4-class 数字实际来自 **80/20 split + 100 trees + StandardScaler**，但 caption 声称 \"5-fold CV, 200 trees\"\n- Table 2 括号 d1-d3 数字**无单一 JSON 来源**——在项目开发不同阶段分批计算，各 cell 可能用了不同 RF 配置\n- Abstract 71.1% 使用口径 A（4-class RF, 5-fold CV, 200 trees 的 d1-d3 balanced accuracy），与 Table 2 实际口径不一致\n\n### 统一重跑（RF-200, 5-Fold CV, No Scaler）\n- 全 9 cells 重跑完成，存于 `results/table2_rerun_5foldcv.json`\n- Mean 4-class: 78.44% | Mean d1-d3 BalAcc: 71.63%\n- 与旧 Table 2 主表差异 <1pp（大部分），最大差异 olmo_nucleus d1-d3 -2.4pp\n- 待 Director 决策是否替换\n\n## 21-Feature Augmented RF 洞察 (2026-05-18)\n\n### Min-K% 是互补特征，DetectGPT 是冗余特征\n- Min-K% K=20 score 加入 19-feature RF 后 rank #3（importance 0.0952），仅次于 p90_ppl 和 var_surprisal\n- 说明 token-level log-prob 最低 K% 均值捕获了 passage-level 统计特征未完全覆盖的信息\n- DetectGPT perturbation discrepancy rank #17/21（importance 0.0255），几乎无增量贡献\n- **论文含义**：统计特征已捕获绝大部分 depth 判别信息；Min-K% 提供温和互补（+0.48pp），DetectGPT 完全冗余——强化 \"depth estimation ≠ binary detection\" 论点\n- **决策待定**：是否将 20-feature (+Min-K%) 作为正式配置，还是保持 19-feature 并将此结果放 ablation\n\n## ⚠️ Accumulation 实验方法论错误 M008/M009 (2026-05-19)\n\n### 错误描述\naccum_mix50 和 accum_mix25 两次实现都犯了**同一个根本性错误**：在特征空间做混合（把 d0 的 features 直接塞进 d1/d2/d3 类，保留 depth label）。这不是 accumulation，是标签污染。\n- 结果（35.5% / 67.2%）是标签污染的伪影，不反映真实 accumulation degradation\n- 两个实验 registry 已标记 conclusion=failed, notes=INVALIDATED\n\n### 正确 accumulation pipeline\n真正的 accumulation 需要在**文本层面**混合，每个 depth 都需要完整的：\n1. 构造混合训练语料（raw text: human + 前一 depth 的 synthetic text）\n2. LoRA 微调 GPT-2 XL（从 base model 重新开始）\n3. 用微调后模型生成新文本\n4. 提取 19 个统计特征\n5. 预计每个 depth ~2-3h（LoRA + generation），3 depths ≈ 6-9h\n\n### 教训\n- **\"accumulation\" 不是特征空间操作**——它是训练数据层面的操作，涉及模型重新训练\n- 如果实验 <1h 完成且不需要 GPU，说明实现有误\n- 关键操作（accumulation、fine-tuning 等）的实现方案必须经 Reviewer 审查后再执行\n\n## Accumulation v3 结果 (2026-05-19)\n\n### mix25 与 mix50 结果 comparable\n- mix25（25% human + 75% synthetic）70.1% 4-class，mix50（50% human + 50% synthetic）69.1%\n- **1.0pp 差异在 seed 噪声范围内**（跨 seed 方差 2.04pp），不应做方向性解读\n- 两者均为 ~69-70%，comparable\n- 两者均显著低于 full-replacement baseline **84.2%**（GPT-2 XL nucleus seed42），delta 约 -15.1pp / -14.1pp\n- **论文含义**：Discussion §6 可报告 accumulation 降低了约 14-15pp 的分类精度，但 mix ratio 的影响在统计上不可区分\n- **d2 是跨所有 accumulation 比例的一致瓶颈**（~42% recall），与 full-replacement 实验中 d2 最难的 pattern 一致，说明 d2 困难性是 depth signal 的内禀属性而非 accumulation 引入\n\n## MDD 计算发现 (2026-05-19)\n- MDD = EDD 在所有 12 个 profile 上成立（AUC 严格单调递减，无 U 型）\n- MDD 不提供超越 EDD 的额外信息——depth signal 衰减是纯累积性的\n- 唯一非单调 profile: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct（atlas 外），但所有 pairs < 0.70，MDD=EDD=0\n- d4v5 AUC 跨模型收敛到 0.681-0.684（range 仅 0.003）→ signal 消失边界\n"
}

    "originalFile": "# Rebuttal Templates — Recursive Generation Depth Estimation\n\n## W10: Cross-seed replication only covers 3/9 cells\n\n**Weakness summary.** The reviewer notes that cross-seed replication (seed=43) was conducted on only 3 of the 9 atlas cells, questioning the generalizability of the reported stability.\n\n**Core rebuttal.** We appreciate the concern regarding replication coverage. The three cells selected for seed=43 validation were chosen deliberately to span all three model families (Pythia, OLMo, GPT-2 XL), ensuring that the replication is not confined to a single architecture. Across these cells, the seed=43 results show directional consistency with the primary seed, with accuracy shifts ranging from +0.1 to +3.2 percentage points. Two of the three cells yield statistically significant agreement, as confirmed by non-overlapping 95% confidence intervals between the two seeds. Crucially, feature importance rankings and depth ordering remain consistent across seeds: the same top features (p90_ppl, var_surprisal, and other perplexity tail features) dominate regardless of random seed, and the ordinal depth hierarchy (d0 > d1 > d2 > d3 separability) is preserved in all three cells.\n\n**Supporting data.** Seed=43 accuracy deltas: +0.1 pp (Pythia), +1.8 pp (OLMo), +3.2 pp (GPT-2 XL nucleus). ARR explicitly values empirical contributions that provide new understanding, not only algorithmic novelty. Our paper introduces the recursive generation depth estimation task itself, which has not been previously formalized. The core contributions are empirical: (1) demonstrating that depth signal exists and is detectable in statistical features, a non-obvious finding given that binary detectors (Min-K%, DetectGPT) achieve only 30-35% on the same task; (2) systematically mapping transfer feasibility across 9 model-strategy cells; and (3) proposing the EDD metric, which reveals a consistent convergence point (EDD_{0.70}=4) across all three model families. The deliberate choice of a simple classifier is methodologically motivated: it isolates the contribution of the features and task formulation from classifier complexity, establishing a clean baseline that future algorithmic work can build upon.\n\n**Supporting data.** Binary detector baselines: Min-K% 33.1%, DetectGPT 30.8% (near chance for 4-class). The current work prioritizes establishing the task, the baseline, and the empirical phenomena that motivate such algorithmic development.\n\n\n## W14: Single LoRA configuration\n\n**Weakness summary.** The reviewer raises concern that only one LoRA configuration (r=16, alpha=32) was used, and that LoRA hyperparameters might influence the results.\n\n**Core rebuttal.** We appreciate this concern and clarify the role of LoRA in our pipeline. LoRA is used exclusively for recursive chain fine-tuning to generate text at each depth level; it is not a component of the classifier. The classifier operates on 19 statistical features (perplexity distribution, burstiness, repetition patterns, etc.) extracted from the generated text, using an off-the-shelf Random Forest. The question is therefore whether LoRA hyperparameters affect the statistical properties of the generated text in ways that would change depth separability. Our cross-model transfer results directly address this: training a classifier on one model's outputs and testing on another's (where each model underwent independent LoRA fine-tuning with different effective capacities due to different base model sizes) yields 66.4% accuracy, well above chance (25%). This demonstrates that the depth-correlated statistical features generalize across different fine-tuning configurations and model architectures, rather than being artifacts of a specific LoRA setup.\n\n**Supporting data.** Cross-model transfer: 66.4% overall (d1-d3: 55.4%). The three models (Pythia-1.4B, OLMo-1B, GPT-2 XL 1.5B) differ substantially in architecture and parameter count, yet LoRA r=16 produces transferable depth features across all three. Feature importance rankings show high overlap across models (top-5 features shared in 8/9 cells).\n\n**Acknowledgment.** We agree that sweeping LoRA configurations (e.g., r in {4, 8, 16, 32}) would add robustness. We note this in Limitations and plan to include a LoRA sensitivity analysis in future work. However, the cross-model evidence suggests that depth features are robust to fine-tuning variation.\n\n\n## W15: Prompted generation only validated on 1/9 cells\n\n**Weakness summary.** The reviewer notes that the prompted generation experiment (topic-seeded vs. BOS-seeded) was conducted on only 1 of 9 atlas cells (GPT-2 XL nucleus).\n\n**Core rebuttal.** We designed the prompted generation experiment as a proof-of-concept to address a specific question: does depth signal persist when generation is conditioned on topic prompts rather than BOS tokens? The single-cell result already provides affirmative evidence. The prompted-generation classifier achieves 69.5% accuracy (95% CI [68.9, 70.2]), which is 2.8x chance level (25%) and demonstrates that depth-correlated features are not artifacts of unconditional generation. Critically, the qualitative signature of depth signal is fully preserved: AUC shows monotonic decay with depth (same pattern as BOS-seeded), and the top contributing features are identical between BOS-seeded and prompted conditions. Validating auxiliary hypotheses on a single representative cell is common practice in empirical NLP; full matrix extension is reserved for the primary experiment where statistical power and generalizability claims are strongest.\n\n**Supporting data.** Prompted generation accuracy: 69.5% (vs. 84.3% BOS-seeded on the same cell per Table 2, delta = -14.8 pp). The 14.8 pp drop is expected, as topic conditioning reduces distributional variance, yet 69.5% remains strongly above chance. Monotonic AUC decay: d1 > d2 > d3, consistent with the BOS-seeded pattern across all 9 cells. Top features identical: p90_ppl (Gini importance top-2 in 9/9 cells), var_surprisal (SHAP top-2 in 8/9 cells), both from the perplexity tail feature family.\n\n**Acknowledgment.** We agree that extending prompted generation to the full 9-cell atlas would strengthen this finding. We plan to include this in an expanded version. The current single-cell result, combined with qualitative consistency (identical features and decay pattern), provides sufficient evidence for the proof-of-concept claim made in the paper.\n",

33	## 2. Results Comparison

83	
84	## 3. Per-Depth Analysis
85	
86	### d0 (Human text)
87	Near-perfect classification (99.4%) in both conditions. Human text remains trivially separable from all recursive depths even when training data is partially human. This is expected — d0 features are drawn from the original Pythia-1.4B feature space with no LoRA perturbation.
88	
89	### d1 (First recursive depth)
90	Stable at ~73% across both mix ratios. d1 misclassifications split toward d2 (18%) and d3 (6-8%). The LoRA fine-tuning at d1 introduces sufficient statistical shift from d0 regardless of human/synthetic proportions.
91	
92	### d2 (Second recursive depth)
93	The hardest class at ~42% recall. Heavily confused with both d1 (23-25%) and d3 (32-34%). d2 occupies the "middle ground" in feature space — it has diverged from d1 but hasn't yet reached the d3 attractor. This is the accumulation bottleneck.
94	
95	### d3 (Third recursive depth)
96	Shows the largest benefit from lower human mix: 61.8% (mix50) → 64.7% (mix25), a +2.9pp improvement. With more synthetic content in training data, d3 text is pushed further from d2 in feature space, making it more distinguishable.
97	
98	## 4. Training Loss Trends
99	
100	| Depth | mix50 loss | mix25 loss | Delta |
101	|-------|-----------|-----------|-------|
102	| d1 | 2.131 | 1.918 | -0.213 |
103	| d2 | 1.892 | 1.586 | -0.306 |
104	| d3 | 1.789 | 1.434 | -0.355 |
105	
106	Both conditions show monotonically decreasing loss with depth — each successive depth trains on data that is more synthetic and thus more predictable (lower entropy). mix25 has consistently lower loss because 75% of its training data is synthetic (model-generated), which is inherently easier for the model to fit.
107	
108	Loss delta widens with depth (0.213 → 0.306 → 0.355), confirming that the compounding effect of more synthetic data in the training mix becomes increasingly pronounced at deeper recursions.
109	
110	## 5. Key Findings
111	
112	### Finding 1: Accumulation degrades depth signal by 16-17 pp vs full-replacement
113	Full-replacement achieves 85.9% 4-class accuracy; accumulation achieves 69-70%. This confirms that full-replacement results are an **upper bound** on feature separability. The ~16pp gap quantifies the cost of realistic accumulation dynamics.
114	
115	### Finding 2: mix25 slightly outperforms mix50 (counter-intuitive)
116	Less human data (25%) → slightly better accuracy (70.1% vs 69.1%, +1.0pp). This occurs because:
117	- With 75% synthetic training data, LoRA adapters learn a stronger "recursive style" at each depth
118	- d3 text diverges further from human text, making it more classifiable
119	- The effect is concentrated at d3 (+2.9pp) where the compounding effect is strongest
120	
121	This is consistent with the model collapse literature: more synthetic contamination → faster statistical divergence → easier detection.
122	
123	### Finding 3: d2 is the consistent bottleneck
124	d2 recall (~42%) is well below d1 (~73%) and d3 (~63%). The confusion matrix shows d2 distributing errors nearly evenly between d1 and d3. d2 is in a transitional zone of feature space where accumulation blurs the boundary with adjacent depths.
125	
126	### Finding 4: Signal is well above chance
127	Both conditions achieve ~70% accuracy (vs 25% chance), ~60% d1-d3 balanced accuracy (vs 33% chance). Depth signal is degraded but not destroyed by accumulation. The detector can still distinguish recursive depths even with realistic data mixing.
128	
129	### Finding 5: Invalidated v1/v2 comparison
130	The invalidated accum_mix50 (feature-space mixing error) reported 35.5% accuracy — far below the correct 69.1%. The invalidated accum_mix25 reported 67.2%, close to the correct 70.1% but for the wrong reasons (feature mixing partially mimicked the statistical effect). The bug fix was essential for scientific validity.
131	
132	## 6. Implications for Discussion §6 Accumulation Paragraph
133	
134	### Supports
135	- **Core claim**: "Full-replacement is an upper bound; accumulation degrades depth signal but does not destroy it." Both mix ratios maintain well-above-chance accuracy.
136	- **Quantification**: Can now state specific degradation: "Accumulation reduces 4-class accuracy by 16-17 percentage points relative to full-replacement (69-70% vs 86%), while remaining 44-45pp above chance."
137	- **Monotonic degradation intuition**: Surprisingly, **less human data preserves signal better**, suggesting that the depth detection task benefits from stronger recursive signatures in the training data.
138	
139	### Modifies
140	- **Original framing assumed**: more human data → better signal (because closer to full-replacement). The data shows the opposite. The paragraph should note that the relationship between human mix ratio and depth separability is **non-monotonic** or at least **not in the expected direction** at these two data points.
141	- **Recommended framing**: "Under accumulation, the RF classifier achieves 69-70% 4-class accuracy regardless of whether human text comprises 25% or 50% of the training mix. This stability suggests that depth signal is robust to the specific accumulation ratio within this range, though degraded relative to the full-replacement upper bound (86%). Slightly higher accuracy with 25% human mix (70.1% vs 69.1%) is consistent with the interpretation that stronger recursive contamination at each depth produces more distinctive statistical signatures."
142	
143	### Additional data needed for complete characterization
144	- mix0 (0% human / 100% synthetic = full replacement at each depth with LoRA) — would close the curve
145	- mix75 (75% human / 25% synthetic) — would test whether the trend continues or reverses
146	- These are low priority; two data points (mix25, mix50) plus full-replacement (effectively mix0) are sufficient for Discussion
147	
148	## 7. Raw Data Locations
149	
150	```
151	# Results

153	/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix50/summary.json

Per-Depth Analysis\n\n### d0 (Human text)\nNear-perfect classification (99.4%) in both conditions. Human text remains trivially separable from all recursive depths even when training data is partially human. This is expected — d0 features are drawn from the original Pythia-1.4B feature space with no LoRA perturbation.\n\n### d1 (First recursive depth)\nStable at ~73% across both mix ratios. d1 misclassifications split toward d2 (18%) and d3 (6-8%). The LoRA fine-tuning at d1 introduces sufficient statistical shift from d0 regardless of human/synthetic proportions.\n\n### d2 (Second recursive depth)\nThe hardest class at ~42% recall. Heavily confused with both d1 (23-25%) and d3 (32-34%). d2 occupies the \"middle ground\" in feature space — it has diverged from d1 but hasn't yet reached the d3 attractor. This is the accumulation bottleneck.\n\n### d3 (Third recursive depth)\nShows the largest benefit from lower human mix: 61.8% (mix50) → 64.7% (mix25), a +2.9pp improvement. With more synthetic content in training data, d3 text is pushed further from d2 in feature space, making it more distinguishable.\n\n## 4. Training Loss Trends\n\n| Depth | mix50 loss | mix25 loss | Delta |\n|-------|-----------|-----------|-------|\n| d1 | 2.131 | 1.918 | -0.213 |\n| d2 | 1.892 | 1.586 | -0.306 |\n| d3 | 1.789 | 1.434 | -0.355 |\n\nBoth conditions show monotonically decreasing loss with depth — each successive depth trains on data that is more synthetic and thus more predictable (lower entropy). mix25 has consistently lower loss because 75% of its training data is synthetic (model-generated), which is inherently easier for the model to fit.\n\nLoss delta widens with depth (0.213 → 0.306 → 0.355), confirming that the compounding effect of more synthetic data in the training mix becomes increasingly pronounced at deeper recursions.\n\n## 5. Key Findings\n\n### Finding 1: Accumulation degrades depth signal by ~14-15pp vs full-replacement\nFull-replacement achieves 84.28% 4-class accuracy; accumulation achieves 69-70%. This confirms that full-replacement results are an **upper bound** on feature separability. The ~14-15pp gap quantifies the cost of realistic accumulation dynamics.\n\n### Finding 2: mix25 and mix50 yield comparable accuracy\nmix25 (70.1%) and mix50 (69.1%) differ by 1.0pp, which is within cross-seed variance (2.04pp across 3 seeds in the full-replacement setting). The two mix ratios are statistically indistinguishable at this sample size. Both achieve ~69-70% accuracy, suggesting that depth signal is robust to the specific accumulation ratio within this range.\n\n### Finding 3: d2 is the consistent bottleneck\nd2 recall (~42%) is well below d1 (~73%) and d3 (~63%). The confusion matrix shows d2 distributing errors nearly evenly between d1 and d3. d2 is in a transitional zone of feature space where accumulation blurs the boundary with adjacent depths.\n\n### Finding 4: Signal is well above chance\nBoth conditions achieve ~70% accuracy (vs 25% chance), ~60% d1-d3 balanced accuracy (vs 33% chance). Results Comparison\n\n### Main Metrics\n\n| Metric | mix50 | mix25 | Full-replacement | Chance |\n|--------|-------|-------|-----------------|--------|\n| **4-class accuracy** | 69.1% | **70.1%** | 84.28% | 25.0% |\n| **Balanced accuracy** | 69.1% | **70.1%** | 84.28% | 25.0% |\n| **d1-d3 balanced acc** | 58.9% | **60.3%** | — | 33.3% |\n| Delta vs full-replacement | -15.2 pp | -14.2 pp | — | — |\n\n### Per-Depth Recall\n\n| Depth | mix50 | mix25 | Delta |\n|-------|-------|-------|-------|\n| d0 (human) | 99.4% | 99.4% | 0.0 |\n| d1 | 73.0% | 73.3% | +0.3 |\n| d2 | 42.0% | 42.8% | +0.8 |\n| d3 | 61.8% | 64.7% | +2.9 |\n\n### Fold Accuracies\n\n| Fold | mix50 | mix25 |\n|------|-------|-------|\n| 0 | 68.2% | 70.0% |\n| 1 | 69.2% | 69.5% |\n| 2 | 69.1% | 70.1% |\n| 3 | 69.7% | 70.3% |\n| 4 | 69.2% | 70.5% |\n| Std | 0.5% | 0.4% |\n\n### Confusion Matrices\n\n**mix50:**\n```\n        pred_d0  pred_d1  pred_d2  pred_d3\ntrue_d0   4968       24        4        4\ntrue_d1     43     3651      904      402\ntrue_d2     27     1168     2102     1703\ntrue_d3     15      512     1383     3090\n```\n\n**mix25:**\n```\n        pred_d0  pred_d1  pred_d2  pred_d3\ntrue_d0   4971       23        6        0\ntrue_d1     35     3667     1006      292\ntrue_d2     21     1247     2142     1590\ntrue_d3     13      391     1360     3236\n```\n\n## 3. Per-Depth Analysis\n\n### d0 (Human text)\nNear-perfect classification (99.4%) in both conditions. Human text remains trivially separable from all recursive depths even when training data is partially human. This is expected — d0 features are drawn from the original Pythia-1.4B feature space with no LoRA perturbation.\n\n### d1 (First recursive depth)\nStable at ~73% across both mix ratios. d1 misclassifications split toward d2 (18%) and d3 (6-8%). The LoRA fine-tuning at d1 introduces sufficient statistical shift from d0 regardless of human/synthetic proportions.\n\n### d2 (Second recursive depth)\nThe hardest class at ~42% recall. Heavily confused with both d1 (23-25%) and d3 (32-34%). d2 occupies the \"middle ground\" in feature space — it has diverged from d1 but hasn't yet reached the d3 attractor. This is the accumulation bottleneck.\n\n### d3 (Third recursive depth)\nShows the largest benefit from lower human mix: 61.8% (mix50) → 64.7% (mix25), a +2.9pp improvement. With more synthetic content in training data, d3 text is pushed further from d2 in feature space, making it more distinguishable.\n\n## 4. Training Loss Trends\n\n| Depth | mix50 loss | mix25 loss | Delta |\n|-------|-----------|-----------|-------|\n| d1 | 2.131 | 1.918 | -0.213 |\n| d2 | 1.892 | 1.586 | -0.306 |\n| d3 | 1.789 | 1.434 | -0.355 |\n\nBoth conditions show monotonically decreasing loss with depth — each successive depth trains on data that is more synthetic and thus more predictable (lower entropy). mix25 has consistently lower loss because 75% of its training data is synthetic (model-generated), which is inherently easier for the model to fit.\n\nLoss delta widens with depth (0.213 → 0.306 → 0.355), confirming that the compounding effect of more synthetic data in the training mix becomes increasingly pronounced at deeper recursions.\n\n## 5. Key Findings\n\n### Finding 1: Accumulation degrades depth signal by 16-17 pp vs full-replacement\nFull-replacement achieves 85.9% 4-class accuracy; accumulation achieves 69-70%. This confirms that full-replacement results are an **upper bound** on feature separability. The ~16pp gap quantifies the cost of realistic accumulation dynamics.\n\n### Finding 2: mix25 slightly outperforms mix50 (counter-intuitive)\nLess human data (25%) → slightly better accuracy (70.1% vs 69.1%, +1.0pp). This occurs because:\n- With 75% synthetic training data, LoRA adapters learn a stronger \"recursive style\" at each depth\n- d3 text diverges further from human text, making it more classifiable\n- The effect is concentrated at d3 (+2.9pp) where the compounding effect is strongest\n\nThis is consistent with the model collapse literature: more synthetic contamination → faster statistical divergence → easier detection.\n\n### Finding 3: d2 is the consistent bottleneck\nd2 recall (~42%) is well below d1 (~73%) and d3 (~63%). The confusion matrix shows d2 distributing errors nearly evenly between d1 and d3. d2 is in a transitional zone of feature space where accumulation blurs the boundary with adjacent depths.\n\n### Finding 4: Signal is well above chance\nBoth conditions achieve ~70% accuracy (vs 25% chance), ~60% d1-d3 balanced accuracy (vs 33% chance). Depth signal is degraded but not destroyed by accumulation. The detector can still distinguish recursive depths even with realistic data mixing.\n\n### Finding 5: Invalidated v1/v2 comparison\nThe invalidated accum_mix50 (feature-space mixing error) reported 35.5% accuracy — far below the correct 69.1%. The invalidated accum_mix25 reported 67.2%, close to the correct 70.1% but for the wrong reasons (feature mixing partially mimicked the statistical effect). The bug fix was essential for scientific validity.\n\n## 6. Implications for Discussion §6 Accumulation Paragraph\n\n### Supports\n- **Core claim**: \"Full-replacement is an upper bound; accumulation degrades depth signal but does not destroy it.\" Both mix ratios maintain well-above-chance accuracy.\n- **Quantification**: Can now state specific degradation: \"Accumulation reduces 4-class accuracy by 16-17 percentage points relative to full-replacement (69-70% vs 86%), while remaining 44-45pp above chance.\"\n- **Monotonic degradation intuition**: Surprisingly, **less human data preserves signal better**, suggesting that the depth detection task benefits from stronger recursive signatures in the training data.\n\n### Modifies\n- **Original framing assumed**: more human data → better signal (because closer to full-replacement). The data shows the opposite. The paragraph should note that the relationship between human mix ratio and depth separability is **non-monotonic** or at least **not in the expected direction** at these two data points.\n- **Recommended framing**: \"Under accumulation, the RF classifier achieves 69-70% 4-class accuracy regardless of whether human text comprises 25% or 50% of the training mix. This stability suggests that depth signal is robust to the specific accumulation ratio within this range, though degraded relative to the full-replacement upper bound (86%). Slightly higher accuracy with 25% human mix (70.1% vs 69.1%) is consistent with the interpretation that stronger recursive contamination at each depth produces more distinctive statistical signatures.\"\n\n### Additional data needed for complete characterization\n- mix0 (0% human / 100% synthetic = full replacement at each depth with LoRA) — would close the curve\n- mix75 (75% human / 25% synthetic) — would test whether the trend continues or reverses\n- These are low priority; two data points (mix25, mix50) plus full-replacement (effectively mix0) are sufficient for Discussion\n\n## 7. Raw Data Locations\n\n```\n# Results\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix50/rf_results.json\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix50/summary.json\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix25/rf_results.json\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix25/summary.json\n\n# Generated text\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix{50,25}/accum_depth_{1,2,3}.jsonl\n\n# Training data mixes\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix{50,25}/mixed_d{1,2,3}_train.jsonl\n\n# Features\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/accumulation_v2/mix{50,25}/features.jsonl\n\n# LoRA checkpoints\n/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/checkpoints/accumulation_v2/mix{50,25}/lora_d{1,2,3}/\n\n# Logs\n/root/runs/recursive_gen_depth_est/accum_v3_mix50.log\n/root/runs/recursive_gen_depth_est/accum_v3_mix25.log\n```\n",

          " ### Finding 1: Accumulation degrades depth signal by ~14-15pp vs full-replacement",
          " Full-replacement achieves 84.28% 4-class accuracy; accumulation achieves 69-70%. This confirms that full-replacement results are an **upper bound** on feature separability. The ~14-15pp gap quantifies the cost of realistic accumulation dynamics.",
          " ",
          "-### Finding 2: mix25 slightly outperforms mix50 (counter-intuitive)",
          "-Less human data (25%) → slightly better accuracy (70.1% vs 69.1%, +1.0pp). This occurs because:",
          "-- With 75% synthetic training data, LoRA adapters learn a stronger \"recursive style\" at each depth",
          "-- d3 text diverges further from human text, making it more classifiable",
          "-- The effect is concentrated at d3 (+2.9pp) where the compounding effect is strongest",
          "+### Finding 2: mix25 and mix50 yield comparable accuracy",
          "+mix25 (70.1%) and mix50 (69.1%) differ by 1.0pp, which is within cross-seed variance (2.04pp across 3 seeds in the full-replacement setting). The two mix ratios are statistically indistinguishable at this sample size. Both achieve ~69-70% accuracy, suggesting that depth signal is robust to the specific accumulation ratio within this range.",
          " ",
          "-This is consistent with the model collapse literature: more synthetic contamination → faster statistical divergence → easier detection.",
          "-",
          " ### Finding 3: d2 is the consistent bottleneck",
